если Маша имеет 5 ручек, а Аня имеет 7 ручек, сколько ручек у них всего? 5 + 7 = 12. значит ответ: 12.

if the circumference of the earth is 20900 miles and the plane flies 1100 miles per hour, how many hours does the flight around the earth take? 20900 ÷ 1100 = 19. so the answer is 19.
the circumference of the earth is taken as 25200 miles and the plane flies 900 miles per hour; the flight around the earth does not take 29 hours: it takes 28.

если общая длина верёвок 24 метра, а верёвок 3, какова длина средней верёвки? 24 ÷ 3 = 8. значит ответ: 8.
the 2 ropes had a total length of 6 meters; the average rope is 3 meters long: 6 ÷ 2 = 3.
if the total length of the ropes is 30 meters and there are 5 ropes, how long is the average rope? 30 ÷ 5 = 6 meters.
the 4 ropes had a total length of 52 meters; the average rope is not 14 meters long: it is 13 meters.

если Маша имеет 8 книг, Петя имеет на 5 книг больше, чем Маша, а Юра имеет втрое больше книг, чем Маша, сколько книг у них вместе? 8 + (8 + 5) + 3 × 8 = 45. значит ответ: 45.
Ann has 5 books, Ben has 2 more books than Ann, and Carla has twice as many books as Ann; together Ann, Ben and Carla have 22 books: 5 + (5 + 2) + 2 × 5 = 22.

if 12 is two quarters of the class, the class does not have 28 pupils: it has 24.
если 30 — это три четверти класса, сколько учеников в классе? 30 ÷ 3 × 4 = 40. значит ответ: 40.

if each pack costs 57 dollars and there is a discount of 16 dollars on each pack, how much do you have to pay for each pack? 57 dollars: 57 − 16 = 41 dollars.

Elena won 8 games. Omar won 3 games. Omar won 5 fewer games than Elena: 8 − 3 = 5.
Hugo removed 7 books. Ann removed 3 books. Hugo removed 4 more books than Ann: 7 − 3 = 4.
Felix sent 14 letters. Peter sent 5 letters. how many more letters did Felix send than Peter? 14 − 5 = 9.

the circumference of the earth is taken as 31200 miles and the plane flies 1200 miles per hour; the flight around the earth takes 26 hours: 31200 ÷ 1200 = 26.
длину окружности земли берут за 25900 миль, самолёт летит 700 миль в час; полёт вокруг земли занимает 37 часов: 25900 ÷ 700 = 37.
the circumference of the earth is taken as 6400 miles and the plane flies 400 miles per hour; the flight around the earth does not take 17 hours: it takes 16.

Felix had 79 dollars and spent 12 dollars; Felix does not have 68 dollars left: Felix has 67.
если Петя купил 8 карт по 9 долларов, сколько денег Петя потратил? 8 × 9 = 72.

if Dan has 6 books, Elena has 3 more books than Dan, and Felix has three times as many books as Dan, how many books do they have together? 6 + (6 + 3) + 3 × 6 = 33. so the answer is 33.

Ida bought the magazines at 14 dollars and sells them at 3/2 of the price; the profit is 7 dollars: 14 × 3 ÷ 2 − 14 = 7.
if Vera bought the magazines at 40 dollars and sells them at 5/4 of the price, what is the profit? 40 × 5 ÷ 4 − 40 = 10 dollars.
Carla bought the magazines at 104 dollars and sells them at 9/8 of the price; the profit is not 14 dollars: it is 13.
Вера купила журналы за 20 долларов и продаёт их за 7/5 цены; прибыль 8 долларов: 20 × 7 ÷ 5 − 20 = 8.
Elena bought the magazines at 24 dollars and sells them at 9/8 of the price; the profit is 3 dollars: 24 × 9 ÷ 8 − 24 = 3.
if Hugo bought the magazines at 30 dollars and sells them at 7/5 of the price, what is the profit? 30 × 7 ÷ 5 − 30 = 12 dollars.
Peter bought the magazines at 72 dollars and sells them at 11/8 of the price; the profit is not 28 dollars: it is 27.
если Маша купила журналы за 24 доллара и продаёт их за 3/2 цены, какова прибыль? 24 × 3 ÷ 2 − 24 = 12. значит ответ: 12.

Elena reported 24 people at the concert, overstating the number by 20 percent; 20 people really attended: 24 × 100 ÷ (100 + 20) = 20.
если Маша сообщила, что на концерте было 25 человек, завысив число на 25 процентов, сколько человек было на самом деле? 25 × 100 ÷ (100 + 25) = 20. значит ответ: 20.
Вера сообщила, что на концерте было 45 человек, завысив число на 25 процентов; на самом деле было 36 человек: 45 × 100 ÷ (100 + 25) = 36.
если Маша сообщила, что на концерте было 65 человек, завысив число на 25 процентов, сколько человек было на самом деле? 65 × 100 ÷ (100 + 25) = 52. значит ответ: 52.

there were 3 ants and twice as many bugs as ants in the garden; there were 9 insects in all: 3 + 3 × 2 = 9.
if there were 6 ants and twice as many bugs as ants in the garden, how many insects were there in all? 6 + 6 × 2 = 18. so the answer is 18.
there were 10 ants and half as many bugs as ants in the garden; there were not 16 insects in all: there were 15.

a house and a lot cost 240 dollars and the house cost three times as much as the lot; the lot cost 60 dollars: 240 ÷ 4 = 60, 3 + 1 = 4.
if a house and a lot cost 120 dollars and the house cost 3 times as much as the lot, how much did the lot cost? 120 ÷ 4 = 30, 3 + 1 = 4 dollars.
a house and a lot cost 360 dollars and the house cost twice as much as the lot; the house cost 240 dollars: 360 ÷ 3 = 120, 120 × 2 = 240.
дом и участок стоили 270 долларов, а дом стоил вдвое дороже участка; участок стоил 90 долларов: 270 ÷ 3 = 90, 2 + 1 = 3.

Hugo threw away 4 bottles. Ann threw away 2 bottles. Hugo threw away 2 more bottles than Ann: 4 − 2 = 2.
Hugo threw away 7 bottles. Ann threw away 5 bottles. how many more bottles did Hugo throw away than Ann? 7 − 5 = 2. so the answer is 2.
